{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/state.ts",
  "content": "import type { ScoeGlobalConfig, ScoeCommandConfig, HealthStatus, LinkTestResult } from './types';\n\n// --- State ---\n\nexport interface CommandIngressState {\n  readonly commandReceiveCount: number;\n  readonly commandSuccessCount: number;\n  readonly commandErrorCount: number;\n\n  readonly loadedSatelliteId: string;\n  readonly scoeFramesLoaded: boolean;\n  readonly healthStatus: HealthStatus;\n  readonly linkTestResult: LinkTestResult;\n  readonly lastCommandCode: string;\n  readonly receiveCommandSuccess: boolean;\n\n  readonly runtimeSeconds: number;\n  readonly satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: number;\n  readonly lastErrorReason: string;\n\n  readonly globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig;\n  readonly activeCommandConfigs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[];\n}\n\n// --- Reader ---\n\nexport interface CommandIngressStateReader {\n  getSnapshot(): CommandIngressState;\n  globalConfig(): ScoeGlobalConfig;\n  readonly loadedSatelliteId: string;\n  readonly scoeFramesLoaded: boolean;\n}\n\n// --- Writer ---\n\nexport interface CommandIngressStateWriter {\n  setLoaded(satelliteId: string, configs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[]): void;\n  resetRuntimeState(): void;\n  updateStatus(patch: Partial<CommandIngressState>): void;\n  incrementReceiveCount(): void;\n  incrementSuccessCount(): void;\n  incrementErrorCount(reason: string): void;\n  tickSecond(): void;\n}\n\n// --- Factory ---\n\nexport interface CommandIngressStateContainer {\n  readonly reader: CommandIngressStateReader;\n  readonly writer: CommandIngressStateWriter;\n}\n\nexport function createCommandIngressState(\n  globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig,\n): CommandIngressStateContainer {\n  let state: CommandIngressState = {\n    commandReceiveCount: 0,\n    commandSuccessCount: 0,\n    commandErrorCount: 0,\n    loadedSatelliteId: '',\n    scoeFramesLoaded: false,\n    healthStatus: 'unknown',\n    linkTestResult: 'unknown',\n    lastCommandCode: '',\n    receiveCommandSuccess: false,\n    runtimeSeconds: 0,\n    satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,\n    lastErrorReason: '',\n    globalConfig,\n    activeCommandConfigs: [],\n  };\n\n  const reader: CommandIngressStateReader = {\n    getSnapshot() {\n      return { ...state };\n    },\n    globalConfig() {\n      return state.globalConfig;\n    },\n    get loadedSatelliteId() {\n      return state.loadedSatelliteId;\n    },\n    get scoeFramesLoaded() {\n      return state.scoeFramesLoaded;\n    },\n  };\n\n  const writer: CommandIngressStateWriter = {\n    setLoaded(satelliteId, configs) {\n      state = {\n        ...state,\n        loadedSatelliteId: satelliteId,\n        scoeFramesLoaded: true,\n        satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,\n        activeCommandConfigs: [...configs],\n      };\n    },\n\n    resetRuntimeState() {\n      state = {\n        ...state,\n        scoeFramesLoaded: false,\n        loadedSatelliteId: '',\n        satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,\n        commandReceiveCount: 0,\n        commandSuccessCount: 0,\n        commandErrorCount: 0,\n        healthStatus: 'unknown',\n        linkTestResult: 'unknown',\n        receiveCommandSuccess: false,\n        lastCommandCode: '',\n        lastErrorReason: '',\n        activeCommandConfigs: [],\n      };\n    },\n\n    updateStatus(patch) {\n      state = { ...state, ...patch };\n    },\n\n    incrementReceiveCount() {\n      state = {\n        ...state,\n        commandReceiveCount: state.commandReceiveCount + 1,\n      };\n    },\n\n    incrementSuccessCount() {\n      state = {\n        ...state,\n        commandSuccessCount: state.commandSuccessCount + 1,\n        receiveCommandSuccess: true,\n      };\n    },\n\n    incrementErrorCount(reason) {\n      state = {\n        ...state,\n        commandErrorCount: state.commandErrorCount + 1,\n        lastErrorReason: reason,\n        receiveCommandSuccess: false,\n      };\n    },\n\n    tickSecond() {\n      let next = {\n        ...state,\n        runtimeSeconds: state.runtimeSeconds + 1,\n        receiveCommandSuccess: false,\n      };\n\n      if (!next.scoeFramesLoaded) {\n        next = {\n          ...next,\n          commandReceiveCount: 0,\n          commandSuccessCount: 0,\n          commandErrorCount: 0,\n          healthStatus: 'unknown',\n          linkTestResult: 'unknown',\n        };\n      } else {\n        next = {\n          ...next,\n          satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: next.satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds + 1,\n        };\n      }\n\n      state = next;\n    },\n  };\n\n  return { reader, writer };\n}\n"
}

readonly scoeFramesLoaded: boolean;\n  readonly healthStatus: string;\n  readonly linkTestResult: string;\n  readonly lastCommandCode: string;\n  readonly receiveCommandSuccess: boolean;\n  readonly {\n  return {\n    commandReceiveCount: state.commandReceiveCount,\n    commandSuccessCount: state.commandSuccessCount,\n    commandErrorCount: state.commandErrorCount,\n    runtimeSeconds: state.runtimeSeconds,\n    satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: state.satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds,\n    lastErrorReason: state.lastErrorReason,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function selectScoeRuntimeStatus(\n  state: selectScoeFramesLoaded(state: CommandIngressState): boolean {\n  return state.scoeFramesLoaded;\n}\n\nexport function selectLoadedSatelliteId(state: CommandIngressState): string {\n  return {
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter.ts",
  "content": "import type { ProtocolAdapter, ParsedCommand } from '../core';\nimport type {\n  ScoeGlobalConfig,\n  ScoeCommandConfig,\n  ChecksumConfig,\n  ScoeCommandParam,\n} from '../core';\nimport type { CommandIngressStateReader } from '../core';\nimport type { TransportEventSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';\nimport type { ConsumerResult } from '@/runtime/consumer-chain';\n\nexport interface ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions {\n  readonly globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig;\n  readonly commandConfigs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[];\n  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;\n}\n\nexport function createScoeProtocolAdapter(\n  options: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions,\n): ProtocolAdapter {\n  const { globalConfig, stateReader } = options;\n  const commandConfigs = [...options.commandConfigs];\n\n  function canHandle(event: TransportEventSnapshot): boolean {\n    if (event.kind !== 'data' || event.bytes === undefined) return false;\n\n    const bytes = event.bytes;\n    const fcOffset = globalConfig.functionCodeOffset;\n\n    if (bytes.length < fcOffset + 4) return false;\n\n    const identifierByte = bytes[fcOffset];\n    const expectedIdentifier = hexToByte(globalConfig.scoeIdentifier);\n    if (expectedIdentifier === null || identifierByte !== expectedIdentifier) return false;\n\n    // Fixed 0xAA 0xAA at offset +2 and +3\n    if (bytes[fcOffset + 2] !== 0xaa || bytes[fcOffset + 3] !== 0xaa) return false;\n\n    // Two-phase state machine\n    if (!stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {\n      // Phase 1: only match LOAD_SATELLITE_ID (command code 01)\n      const commandCode = byteToHex(bytes[fcOffset + 1]);\n      if (commandCode !== '01') return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  function parse(event: TransportEventSnapshot): ParsedCommand {\n    const bytes = event.bytes!;\n    const fcOffset = globalConfig.functionCodeOffset;\n\n    const commandCode = byteToHex(bytes[fcOffset + 1]);\n    const commandConfig = commandConfigs.find((c) => c.code === commandCode);\n    if (!commandConfig) {\n      throw new Error(`Unknown SCOE command code: ${commandCode}`);\n    }\n\n    validateChecksums(bytes, commandConfig.checksums);\n\n    const resolvedParams = extractAndResolveParams(bytes, commandConfig.params ?? []);\n\n    return {\n      commandId: `scoe-cmd-${event.id}`,\n      commandCode,\n      commandFunction: commandConfig.function,\n      rawBytes: [...bytes],\n      resolvedParams,\n      commandConfig,\n      connectionId: event.connectionId,\n      occurredAt: event.occurredAt,\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    id: 'scoe-protocol',\n    protocolId: 'scoe-tcp',\n\n    async consume(events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[]): Promise<ConsumerResult> {\n      const consumed: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];\n      const remaining: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];\n\n      for (const event of events) {\n        if (canHandle(event)) {\n          consumed.push(event);\n        } else {\n          remaining.push(event);\n        }\n      }\n\n      return { consumed, remaining };\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// --- Byte-level helpers ---\n\nfunction byteToHex(byte: number): string {\n  return byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0').toUpperCase();\n}\n\nfunction hexToByte(hex: string): number | null {\n  const cleaned = hex.replace(/^0x/i, '');\n  const value = parseInt(cleaned, 16);\n  return Number.isNaN(value) ? null : value;\n}\n\nfunction validateChecksums(\n  bytes: readonly number[],\n  checksums: readonly ChecksumConfig[],\n): void {\n  for (const cs of checksums) {\n    if (!cs.enabled) continue;\n\n    let sum = 0;\n    for (let i = cs.offset; i < cs.offset + cs.length; i++) {\n      sum += bytes[i] ?? 0;\n    }\n    const expected = sum % 256;\n    const actual = bytes[cs.checksumOffset] ?? 0;\n\n    if (expected !== actual) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `Checksum failed at offset ${cs.checksumOffset}: expected ${expected}, got ${actual}`,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction extractAndResolveParams(\n  bytes: readonly number[],\n  params: readonly ScoeCommandParam[],\n): Record<string, string> {\n  const result: Record<string, string> = {};\n\n  for (const param of params) {\n    const slice = bytes.slice(param.offset, param.offset + param.length);\n    const hex = bytesToUpperCaseHex(slice);\n    const normalized = normalizeHex(hex, param.length * 2);\n\n    const matched = param.options.find(\n      (opt) => normalizeHex(opt.receiveCode, param.length * 2) === normalized,\n    );\n\n    result[param.id] = matched?.value ?? hex;\n  }\n\n  return result;\n}\n\nfunction bytesToUpperCaseHex(bytes: readonly number[]): string {\n  return Array.from(bytes)\n    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0').toUpperCase())\n    .join('');\n}\n\nfunction normalizeHex(hex: string, targetLength: number): string {\n  const cleaned = hex.replace(/^0x/i, '').toUpperCase();\n  return cleaned.padStart(targetLength, '0');\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/scripts/migrate-scoe-config.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Migration script: old SCOE config JSON → new ScoeCommandConfig[] JSON\n *\n * Usage: npx tsx rewrite/scripts/migrate-scoe-config.ts <input.json> <output.json>\n *\n * Input format (old): { commands: OldScoeReceiveCommand[], globalConfig: OldScoeGlobalConfig }\n * Output format (new): { commandConfigs: ScoeCommandConfig[], globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig }\n */\n\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'fs';\nimport { resolve } from 'path';\n\n// --- Old types (source) ---\n\ninterface OldHighlightConfigs {\n  [key: string]: unknown;\n}\n\ninterface OldScoeGlobalConfig {\n  scoeIdentifier: string;\n  tcpServerIp: string;\n  tcpServerPort: number;\n  tcpServerAutoConnect?: boolean;\n  udpIpAddress: string;\n  udpPort: number;\n  messageIdentifierOffset: number;\n  sourceIdentifierOffset: number;\n  destinationIdentifierOffset: number;\n  modelIdOffset: number;\n  satelliteIdOffset: number;\n  functionCodeOffset: number;\n  successFrameId?: string;\n  highlightConfigs?: OldHighlightConfigs;\n}\n\ntype OldMatchOperator = 'equal' | 'not_equal' | 'greater_than' | 'less_than' | 'greater_equal' | 'less_equal' | 'contains';\n\ninterface OldChecksumConfig {\n  enabled: boolean;\n  offset: number;\n  length: number;\n  checksumOffset: number;\n}\n\ninterface OldScoeParamOption {\n  label: string;\n  value: string;\n  receiveCode: string;\n}\n\ninterface OldScoeCommandParams {\n  id: string;\n  label: string;\n  value: string;\n  type: 'string' | 'number' | 'boolean';\n  offset: number;\n  length: number;\n  targetInstanceId?: string;\n  targetFieldId?: string;\n  options: OldScoeParamOption[];\n}\n\ninterface OldCompletionConditionOption {\n  operator: OldMatchOperator;\n  matchValue: string;\n}\n\ninterface OldCompletionCondition {\n  id: string;\n  label: string;\n  sourceFrameId: string;\n  sourceFieldId: string;\n  useParam: boolean;\n  targetParamId?: string;\n  targetFixedValue?: string;\n  operator?: OldMatchOperator;\n  options?: OldCompletionConditionOption[];\n}\n\ntype OldScoeCommandFunction =\n  | 'load_satellite_id'\n  | 'unload_satellite_id'\n  | 'health_check'\n  | 'link_check'\n  | 'send_frame'\n  | 'read_file_and_send';\n\ninterface OldSendFrameInstance {\n  id: string;\n  frameId: string;\n  label?: string;\n  description?: string;\n  targetId?: string;\n  fields?: OldSendInstanceField[];\n  [key: string]: unknown;\n}\n\ninterface OldSendInstanceField {\n  id: string;\n  value?: string | number;\n  targetInstanceId?: string;\n  targetFieldId?: string;\n  [key: string]: unknown;\n}\n\ninterface OldScoeReceiveCommand {\n  id: string;\n  label: string;\n  code: string;\n  function: OldScoeCommandFunction;\n  checksums: OldChecksumConfig[];\n  params?: OldScoeCommandParams[];\n  frameInstances?: OldSendFrameInstance[];\n  completionConditions?: OldCompletionCondition[];\n  completionTimeout?: number;\n  successFrameId?: string;\n  sendInterval?: number;\n}\n\n// --- New types (target) ---\n\ntype ComparisonOperator = 'eq' | 'neq' | 'gt' | 'lt' | 'gte' | 'lte' | 'contains' | 'change' | 'any';\n\ninterface NewFieldMapping {\n  fieldId: string;\n  source: 'fixed' | 'param';\n  fixedValue?: string | number;\n  paramId?: string;\n}\n\ninterface NewScoeCommandFrameMapping {\n  frameId: string;\n  instanceId: string;\n  label?: string;\n  description?: string;\n  targetId: string;\n  fieldMappings: NewFieldMapping[];\n}\n\ninterface NewCompletionConditionOption {\n  operator: ComparisonOperator;\n  matchValue: string;\n}\n\ninterface NewCompletionConditionConfig {\n  id: string;\n  label: string;\n  sourceFrameId: string;\n  sourceFieldId: string;\n  useParam: boolean;\n  targetParamId?: string;\n  targetFixedValue?: string;\n  operator?: ComparisonOperator;\n  options?: NewCompletionConditionOption[];\n}\n\ninterface NewScoeGlobalConfig {\n  scoeIdentifier: string;\n  tcpServerIp: string;\n  tcpServerPort: number;\n  tcpServerAutoConnect: boolean;\n  udpIpAddress: string;\n  udpPort: number;\n  udpTargetId: string;\n  messageIdentifierOffset: number;\n  sourceIdentifierOffset: number;\n  destinationIdentifierOffset: number;\n  modelIdOffset: number;\n  satelliteIdOffset: number;\n  functionCodeOffset: number;\n  successFrameId?: string;\n}\n\ninterface NewScoeCommandConfig {\n  id: string;\n  label: string;\n  code: string;\n  function: OldScoeCommandFunction;\n  checksums: OldChecksumConfig[];\n  params?: OldScoeCommandParams[];\n  frameMappings?: NewScoeCommandFrameMapping[];\n  completionConditions?: NewCompletionConditionConfig[];\n  completionTimeout?: number;\n  successFrameId?: string;\n  sendInterval?: number;\n}\n\n// --- Operator mapping ---\n\nconst OPERATOR_MAP: Record<OldMatchOperator, ComparisonOperator> = {\n  equal: 'eq',\n  not_equal: 'neq',\n  greater_than: 'gt',\n  less_than: 'lt',\n  greater_equal: 'gte',\n  less_equal: 'lte',\n  contains: 'contains',\n};\n\nfunction mapOperator(old: OldMatchOperator): ComparisonOperator {\n  return OPERATOR_MAP[old] ?? 'eq';\n}\n\n// --- Migration logic ---\n\nfunction migrateGlobalConfig(old: OldScoeGlobalConfig): NewScoeGlobalConfig {\n  return {\n    scoeIdentifier: old.scoeIdentifier,\n    tcpServerIp: old.tcpServerIp,\n    tcpServerPort: old.tcpServerPort,\n    tcpServerAutoConnect: old.tcpServerAutoConnect ?? true,\n    udpIpAddress: old.udpIpAddress,\n    udpPort: old.udpPort,\n    udpTargetId: 'network:scoe-udp:scoe-udp-remote',\n    messageIdentifierOffset: old.messageIdentifierOffset,\n    sourceIdentifierOffset: old.sourceIdentifierOffset,\n    destinationIdentifierOffset: old.destinationIdentifierOffset,\n    modelIdOffset: old.modelIdOffset,\n    satelliteIdOffset: old.satelliteIdOffset,\n    functionCodeOffset: old.functionCodeOffset,\n    ...(old.successFrameId ? { successFrameId: old.successFrameId } : {}),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction migrateFrameInstances(\n  oldInstances: OldSendFrameInstance[] | undefined,\n  params: OldScoeCommandParams[] | undefined,\n): NewScoeCommandFrameMapping[] | undefined {\n  if (!oldInstances || oldInstances.length === 0) return undefined;\n\n  const paramByTarget = new Map<string, string>();\n  for (const param of params ?? []) {\n    if (param.targetInstanceId && param.targetFieldId) {\n      paramByTarget.set(`${param.targetInstanceId}::${param.targetFieldId}`, param.id);\n    }\n  }\n\n  return oldInstances.map((inst) => {\n    const fieldMappings: NewFieldMapping[] = [];\n\n    for (const field of inst.fields ?? []) {\n      const key = `${inst.id}::${field.id}`;\n      const paramId = paramByTarget.get(key);\n\n      if (paramId) {\n        fieldMappings.push({\n          fieldId: field.id,\n          source: 'param',\n          paramId,\n        });\n      } else if (field.value !== undefined) {\n        fieldMappings.push({\n          fieldId: field.id,\n          source: 'fixed',\n          fixedValue: field.value,\n        });\n      }\n    }\n\n    return {\n      frameId: inst.frameId,\n      instanceId: inst.id,\n      ...(inst.label ? { label: inst.label } : {}),\n      ...(inst.description ? { description: inst.description } : {}),\n      targetId: inst.targetId ?? 'network:scoe-udp:scoe-udp-remote',\n      fieldMappings,\n    };\n  });\n}\n\nfunction migrateCompletionConditions(\n  oldConditions: OldCompletionCondition[] | undefined,\n): NewCompletionConditionConfig[] | undefined {\n  if (!oldConditions || oldConditions.length === 0) return undefined;\n\n  return oldConditions.map((cond) => ({\n    id: cond.id,\n    label: cond.label,\n    sourceFrameId: cond.sourceFrameId,\n    sourceFieldId: cond.sourceFieldId,\n    useParam: cond.useParam,\n    ...(cond.targetParamId ? { targetParamId: cond.targetParamId } : {}),\n    ...(cond.targetFixedValue ? { targetFixedValue: cond.targetFixedValue } : {}),\n    ...(cond.operator ? { operator: mapOperator(cond.operator) } : {}),\n    ...(cond.options\n      ? {\n          options: cond.options.map((opt) => ({\n            operator: mapOperator(opt.operator),\n            matchValue: opt.matchValue,\n          })),\n        }\n      : {}),\n  }));\n}\n\nfunction migrateCommand(old: OldScoeReceiveCommand): NewScoeCommandConfig {\n  return {\n    id: old.id,\n    label: old.label,\n    code: old.code,\n    function: old.function,\n    checksums: old.checksums.map((cs) => ({ ...cs })),\n    params: old.params?.map((p) => ({\n      id: p.id,\n      label: p.label,\n      value: p.value,\n      type: p.type,\n      offset: p.offset,\n      length: p.length,\n      targetInstanceId: p.targetInstanceId,\n      targetFieldId: p.targetFieldId,\n      options: p.options.map((o) => ({ ...o })),\n    })),\n    frameMappings: migrateFrameInstances(old.frameInstances, old.params),\n    completionConditions: migrateCompletionConditions(old.completionConditions),\n    ...(old.completionTimeout ? { completionTimeout: old.completionTimeout } : {}),\n    ...(old.successFrameId ? { successFrameId: old.successFrameId } : {}),\n    ...(old.sendInterval ? { sendInterval: old.sendInterval } : {}),\n  };\n}\n\n// --- Main ---\n\ninterface MigrationInput {\n  commands: OldScoeReceiveCommand[];\n  globalConfig: OldScoeGlobalConfig;\n}\n\ninterface MigrationOutput {\n  commandConfigs: NewScoeCommandConfig[];\n  globalConfig: NewScoeGlobalConfig;\n}\n\nfunction main(): void {\n  const inputPath = process.argv[2];\n  const outputPath = process.argv[3];\n\n  if (!inputPath || !outputPath) {\n    console.error('Usage: npx tsx rewrite/scripts/migrate-scoe-config.ts <input.json> <output.json>');\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n\n  const raw = readFileSync(resolve(inputPath), 'utf-8');\n  const input: MigrationInput = JSON.parse(raw);\n\n  const output: MigrationOutput = {\n    commandConfigs: input.commands.map(migrateCommand),\n    globalConfig: migrateGlobalConfig(input.globalConfig),\n  };\n\n  writeFileSync(resolve(outputPath), JSON.stringify(output, null, 2), 'utf-8');\n  console.log(`Migrated ${output.commandConfigs.length} commands → ${outputPath}`);\n}\n\nmain();\n"
}

CommandIngressStateReader } from '../core';\nimport type { TransportEventSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';\n\n// --- Global config fixture ---\n\nexport const testGlobalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig = {\n  scoeIdentifier: '0x01',\n  tcpServerIp: '0.0.0.0',\n  tcpServerPort: 6000,\n  tcpServerAutoConnect: true,\n  udpIpAddress: '127.0.0.1',\n  udpPort: 7000,\n  udpTargetId: 'network:scoe-udp:scoe-udp-remote',\n  messageIdentifierOffset: 4,\n  sourceIdentifierOffset: 5,\n  destinationIdentifierOffset: 6,\n  modelIdOffset: 7,\n  satelliteIdOffset: 8,\n  functionCodeOffset: 0,\n  successFrameId: 'ack-frame-001',\n};\n\n// --- Command config fixtures ---\n\nexport const loadCommandConfig: ScoeCommandConfig = {\n  id: 'cmd-load',\n  label: 'LOAD_SATELLITE_ID',\n  code: '01',\n  function: 'load_satellite_id',\n  checksums: [],\n  params: [],\n};\n\nexport const unloadCommandConfig: ScoeCommandConfig = {\n  id: 'cmd-unload',\n  label: 'UNLOAD_SATELLITE_ID',\n  code: '02',\n  function: 'unload_satellite_id',\n  checksums: [],\n  params: [],\n};\n\nexport const sendFrameCommandConfig: ScoeCommandConfig = {\n  id: 'cmd-send',\n  label: 'SEND_FRAME',\n  code: '05',\n  function: 'send_frame',\n  checksums: [\n    { enabled: true, offset: 4, length: 8, checksumOffset: 12 },\n  ],\n  params: [\n    {\n      id: 'param-mode',\n      label: 'Mode',\n      value: '',\n      type: 'string',\n      offset: 13,\n      length: 1,\n      options: [\n        { label: 'Mode A', value: 'mode_a', receiveCode: '01' },\n        { label: 'Mode B', value: 'mode_b', receiveCode: '02' },\n      ],\n    },\n  ],\n  frameMappings: [\n    {\n      frameId: 'frame-001',\n      instanceId: 'inst-001',\n      targetId: 'network:scoe-udp:scoe-udp-remote',\n      fieldMappings: [\n        { fieldId: 'field-mode', source: 'param', paramId: 'param-mode' },\n        { fieldId: 'field-fixed', source: 'fixed', fixedValue: 100 },\n      ],\n    },\n  ],\n  completionConditions: [\n    {\n      id: 'cond-001',\n      label: 'Wait for ack',\n      sourceFrameId: 'frame-ack',\n      sourceFieldId: 'field-status',\n      useParam: false,\n      targetFixedValue: '1',\n      operator: 'eq',\n    },\n  ],\n  completionTimeout: 5000,\n  sendInterval: 100,\n};\n\nexport const testCommandConfigs: ScoeCommandConfig[] = [\n  loadCommandConfig,\n  unloadCommandConfig,\n  sendFrameCommandConfig,\n];\n\n// --- State reader fixture ---\n\nexport function createFakeStateReader(\n  overrides: Partial<CommandIngressState> = {},\n): CommandIngressStateReader {\n  const defaults: CommandIngressState = {\n    commandReceiveCount: 0,\n    commandSuccessCount: 0,\n    commandErrorCount: 0,\n    loadedSatelliteId: '',\n    scoeFramesLoaded: false,\n    healthStatus: 'unknown',\n    linkTestResult: 'unknown',\n    lastCommandCode: '',\n    receiveCommandSuccess: false,\n    runtimeSeconds: 0,\n    satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,\n    lastErrorReason: '',\n    globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n    activeCommandConfigs: [],\n  };\n\n  const state = { ...defaults, ...overrides };\n  return {\n    getSnapshot() { return state; },\n    globalConfig() { return state.globalConfig; },\n    get loadedSatelliteId() { return state.loadedSatelliteId; },\n    get scoeFramesLoaded() { return state.scoeFramesLoaded; },\n  };\n}\n\n// --- Transport event builder ---\n\nlet nextEventId = 1;\n\nexport function buildDataEvent(bytes: number[], connectionId = 'conn-tcp-1'): TransportEventSnapshot {\n  return {\n    id: `evt-${nextEventId++}`,\n    kind: 'data',\n    connectionId,\n    occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    bytes,\n    byteLength: bytes.length,\n  };\n}\n\n// --- SCOE frame byte builder ---\n\nexport function buildScoeFrame(\n  commandCode: number,\n  extraBytes: number[] = [],\n  config: ScoeGlobalConfig = testGlobalConfig,\n): number[] {\n  const fcOffset = config.functionCodeOffset;\n  const identByte = parseInt(config.scoeIdentifier.replace(/^0x/i, ''), 16);\n\n  const frame: number[] = [];\n  // Ensure we have enough room for the function code area\n  while (frame.length < fcOffset + 4) {\n    frame.push(0);\n  }\n\n  frame[fcOffset] = identByte;\n  frame[fcOffset + 1] = commandCode;\n  frame[fcOffset + 2] = 0xaa;\n  frame[fcOffset + 3] = 0xaa;\n\n  return [...frame, ...extraBytes];\n}\n\nexport function buildScoeFrameWithChecksum(\n  commandCode: number,\n  checksumCfg: ChecksumConfig,\n  extraBytes: number[] = [],\n  config: ScoeGlobalConfig = testGlobalConfig,\n): number[] {\n  const frame = buildScoeFrame(commandCode, extraBytes, config);\n\n  // Ensure frame is long enough for checksum area + extra\n  while (frame.length <= checksumCfg.checksumOffset) {\n    frame.push(0);\n  }\n\n  // Calculate checksum\n  let sum = 0;\n  for (let i = checksumCfg.offset; i < checksumCfg.offset + checksumCfg.length; i++) {\n    sum += frame[i] ?? 0;\n  }\n  frame[checksumCfg.checksumOffset] = sum % 256;\n\n  return frame;\n}\n\nexport function resetFixtureIds(): void {\n  nextEventId = 1;\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { describe, expect, it, beforeEach } from 'vitest';\nimport { createScoeProtocolAdapter } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';\nimport {\n  testGlobalConfig,\n  testCommandConfigs,\n  sendFrameCommandConfig,\n  createFakeStateReader,\n  buildDataEvent,\n  buildScoeFrame,\n  buildScoeFrameWithChecksum,\n  resetFixtureIds,\n} from '../fixtures/command-ingress-fixtures';\nimport type { TransportEventSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';\n\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  resetFixtureIds();\n});\n\n// --- C8: Function code validation ---\n\ndescribe('ScoeProtocolAdapter canHandle – function code validation', () => {\n  it('accepts a valid SCOE frame with matching identifier + 0xAA0xAA', () => {\n    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: true });\n    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n      commandConfigs: testCommandConfigs,\n      stateReader,\n    });\n\n    const bytes = buildScoeFrame(0x05);\n    const event = buildDataEvent(bytes);\n    expect(adapter.consume([event])).resolves.toEqual(\n      expect.objectContaining({\n        consumed: [event],\n        remaining: [],\n      }),\n    );\n  });\n\n  it('rejects a frame with wrong SCOE identifier', () => {\n    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: true });\n    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n      commandConfigs: testCommandConfigs,\n      stateReader,\n    });\n\n    // Use identifier 0x02 instead of expected 0x01\n    const bytes = buildScoeFrame(0x05, [], { ...testGlobalConfig, scoeIdentifier: '0x02' });\n    // But build with 0x02 ident in the frame, check against 0x01 config\n    const frame: number[] = [0x02, 0x05, 0xaa, 0xaa];\n    const event = buildDataEvent(frame);\n    expect(adapter.consume([event])).resolves.toEqual(\n      expect.objectContaining({\n        consumed: [],\n        remaining: [event],\n      }),\n    );\n  });\n\n  it('rejects a frame missing 0xAA0xAA fixed bytes', () => {\n    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: true });\n    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n      commandConfigs: testCommandConfigs,\n      stateReader,\n    });\n\n    const frame = [0x01, 0x05, 0x00, 0x00];\n    const event = buildDataEvent(frame);\n    expect(adapter.consume([event])).resolves.toEqual(\n      expect.objectContaining({\n        consumed: [],\n        remaining: [event],\n      }),\n    );\n  });\n\n  it('rejects a frame that is too short for function code area', () => {\n    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: true });\n    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n      commandConfigs: testCommandConfigs,\n      stateReader,\n    });\n\n    const event = buildDataEvent([0x01, 0x05]);\n    expect(adapter.consume([event])).resolves.toEqual(\n      expect.objectContaining({\n        consumed: [],\n        remaining: [event],\n      }),\n    );\n  });\n\n  it('rejects non-data events', () => {\n    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: true });\n    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n      commandConfigs: testCommandConfigs,\n      stateReader,\n    });\n\n    const event: TransportEventSnapshot = {\n      id: 'evt-1',\n      kind: 'connected',\n      connectionId: 'conn-1',\n      occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    };\n    expect(adapter.consume([event])).resolves.toEqual(\n      expect.objectContaining({\n        consumed: [],\n        remaining: [event],\n      }),\n    );\n  });\n});\n\n// --- C9: Checksum validation ---\n\ndescribe('ScoeProtocolAdapter parse – checksum validation', () => {\n  it('accepts a frame with valid checksum', async () => {\n    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: true });\n    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n      commandConfigs: [sendFrameCommandConfig],\n      stateReader,\n    });\n\n    const cs = sendFrameCommandConfig.checksums[0];\n    const bytes = buildScoeFrameWithChecksum(0x05, cs, [\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,  // padding bytes\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,  // more padding\n      0x00,                    // param area\n    ]);\n    const event = buildDataEvent(bytes);\n\n    const result = await adapter.consume([event]);\n    expect(result.consumed).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(result.remaining).toHaveLength(0);\n  });\n\n  it('throws on invalid checksum', async () => {\n    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: true });\n    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n      commandConfigs: [sendFrameCommandConfig],\n      stateReader,\n    });\n\n    const cs = sendFrameCommandConfig.checksums[0];\n    const bytes = buildScoeFrameWithChecksum(0x05, cs, [\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00,\n    ]);\n    // Corrupt the checksum byte\n    bytes[cs.checksumOffset] = 0xff;\n    const event = buildDataEvent(bytes);\n\n    // consume() should still categorize the event (canHandle passes), but parse throws\n    // In W1, consume doesn't call parse — it only checks canHandle\n    // So this event is consumed but parse would fail when called externally\n    const result = await adapter.consume([event]);\n    expect(result.consumed).toHaveLength(1);\n  });\n\n  it('skips disabled checksums', async () => {\n    const cmdWithDisabledCs: typeof sendFrameCommandConfig = {\n      ...sendFrameCommandConfig,\n      checksums: [{ enabled: false, offset: 4, length: 8, checksumOffset: 12 }],\n    };\n    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: true });\n    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n      commandConfigs: [cmdWithDisabledCs],\n      stateReader,\n    });\n\n    const bytes = buildScoeFrame(0x05, [\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00,\n    ]);\n    const event = buildDataEvent(bytes);\n\n    const result = await adapter.consume([event]);\n    expect(result.consumed).toHaveLength(1);\n  });\n});\n\n// --- C10: Parameter extraction and resolve ---\n\ndescribe('ScoeProtocolAdapter parse – parameter resolve', () => {\n  it('resolves param by matching receiveCode to option value', () => {\n    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: true });\n    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n      commandConfigs: [sendFrameCommandConfig],\n      stateReader,\n    });\n\n    // Access internal parse via a test-only approach\n    // The adapter exposes consume(), but we can test parse through the parsed command\n    // by examining the consume result side effects\n    // For W1, consume() does not parse — it only checks canHandle\n    // So we test the helper functions directly via export\n\n    // Since parse is internal, we test it by calling it via the exported module\n    // In W1, we verify through integration — the event is consumed\n    const cs = sendFrameCommandConfig.checksums[0];\n    const bytes = buildScoeFrameWithChecksum(0x05, cs, [\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x02, // param at offset 13, length 1 → hex \"02\" → receiveCode \"02\" → value \"mode_b\"\n    ]);\n    const event = buildDataEvent(bytes);\n\n    // W1: consume only categorizes, doesn't parse\n    expect(adapter.consume([event])).resolves.toEqual(\n      expect.objectContaining({ consumed: [event] }),\n    );\n  });\n});\n\n// --- C11: Two-phase state machine ---\n\ndescribe('ScoeProtocolAdapter canHandle – two-phase state machine', () => {\n  it('phase 1: only accepts LOAD command when scoeFramesLoaded=false', async () => {\n    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: false });\n    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n      commandConfigs: testCommandConfigs,\n      stateReader,\n    });\n\n    const loadEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x01)); // LOAD\n    const sendEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x05)); // SEND_FRAME\n    const unloadEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x02)); // UNLOAD\n\n    const result = await adapter.consume([loadEvent, sendEvent, unloadEvent]);\n    expect(result.consumed).toEqual([loadEvent]);\n    expect(result.remaining).toEqual([sendEvent, unloadEvent]);\n  });\n\n  it('phase 2: accepts all commands when scoeFramesLoaded=true', async () => {\n    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: true });\n    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n      commandConfigs: testCommandConfigs,\n      stateReader,\n    });\n\n    const loadEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x01));\n    const sendEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x05));\n    const unloadEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x02));\n\n    const result = await adapter.consume([loadEvent, sendEvent, unloadEvent]);\n    expect(result.consumed).toEqual([loadEvent, sendEvent, unloadEvent]);\n    expect(result.remaining).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('passes non-SCOE events through as remaining', async () => {\n    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: true });\n    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,\n      commandConfigs: testCommandConfigs,\n      stateReader,\n    });\n\n    const scoeEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x01));\n    const normalEvent = buildDataEvent([0xDE, 0xAD, 0xBE, 0xEF, 0x00, 0x00]);\n    const normalEvent2 = buildDataEvent([0x10, 0x20, 0x30, 0x40, 0x50, 0x60]);\n\n    const result = await adapter.consume([scoeEvent, normalEvent, normalEvent2]);\n    expect(result.consumed).toEqual([scoeEvent]);\n    expect(result.remaining).toEqual([normalEvent, normalEvent2]);\n  });\n});\n\n// --- State container tests (C4-C5) ---\n\ndescribe('CommandIngressState container', () => {\n  it('starts with correct initial values', async () => {\n    const { createCommandIngressState } = await import('../core/state');\n    const { reader } = createCommandIngressState(testGlobalConfig);\n\n    expect(reader.scoeFramesLoaded).toBe(false);\n    expect(reader.loadedSatelliteId).toBe('');\n    const snap = reader.getSnapshot();\n    expect(snap.commandReceiveCount).toBe(0);\n    expect(snap.runtimeSeconds).toBe(0);\n    expect(snap.healthStatus).toBe('unknown');\n  });\n\n  it('setLoaded transitions to phase 2', async () => {\n    const { createCommandIngressState } = await import('../core/state');\n    const { reader, writer } = createCommandIngressState(testGlobalConfig);\n\n    writer.setLoaded('SAT-01', testCommandConfigs);\n    expect(reader.scoeFramesLoaded).toBe(true);\n    expect(reader.loadedSatelliteId).toBe('SAT-01');\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().activeCommandConfigs).toEqual(testCommandConfigs);\n  });\n\n  it('resetRuntimeState clears all runtime data', async () => {\n    const { createCommandIngressState } = await import('../core/state');\n    const { reader, writer } = createCommandIngressState(testGlobalConfig);\n\n    writer.setLoaded('SAT-01', testCommandConfigs);\n    writer.incrementReceiveCount();\n    writer.incrementSuccessCount();\n\n    writer.resetRuntimeState();\n    expect(reader.scoeFramesLoaded).toBe(false);\n    expect(reader.loadedSatelliteId).toBe('');\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().commandReceiveCount).toBe(0);\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().commandSuccessCount).toBe(0);\n  });\n\n  it('tickSecond increments runtimeSeconds and resets flags', async () => {\n    const { createCommandIngressState } = await import('../core/state');\n    const { reader, writer } = createCommandIngressState(testGlobalConfig);\n\n    writer.incrementSuccessCount();\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().receiveCommandSuccess).toBe(true);\n\n    writer.tickSecond();\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().runtimeSeconds).toBe(1);\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().receiveCommandSuccess).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it('tickSecond clears statistics when not loaded', async () => {\n    const { createCommandIngressState } = await import('../core/state');\n    const { reader, writer } = createCommandIngressState(testGlobalConfig);\n\n    // Not loaded — set some counts\n    writer.incrementReceiveCount();\n    writer.incrementErrorCount('test error');\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().commandReceiveCount).toBe(1);\n\n    writer.tickSecond();\n    // When not loaded, tick clears statistics\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().commandReceiveCount).toBe(0);\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().commandErrorCount).toBe(0);\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().healthStatus).toBe('unknown');\n  });\n\n  it('tickSecond increments satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds when loaded', async () => {\n    const { createCommandIngressState } = await import('../core/state');\n    const { reader, writer } = createCommandIngressState(testGlobalConfig);\n\n    writer.setLoaded('SAT-01', testCommandConfigs);\n    writer.tickSecond();\n    writer.tickSecond();\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds).toBe(2);\n  });\n\n  it('incrementErrorCount updates error state', async () => {\n    const { createCommandIngressState } = await import('../core/state');\n    const { reader, writer } = createCommandIngressState(testGlobalConfig);\n\n    writer.incrementErrorCount('timeout');\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().commandErrorCount).toBe(1);\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().lastErrorReason).toBe('timeout');\n    expect(reader.getSnapshot().receiveCommandSuccess).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n\n// --- Selector tests (C6) ---\n\ndescribe('Command ingress selectors', () => {\n  it('selectCommandIngressSnapshot returns deep copy', async () => {\n    const { createCommandIngressState } = await import('../core/state');\n    const { selectCommandIngressSnapshot } = await import('../selectors');\n    const { reader, writer } = createCommandIngressState(testGlobalConfig);\n\n    writer.setLoaded('SAT-01', testCommandConfigs);\n    const state = reader.getSnapshot();\n    const snap = selectCommandIngressSnapshot(state);\n\n    expect(snap.scoeFramesLoaded).toBe(true);\n    expect(snap.loadedSatelliteId).toBe('SAT-01');\n    // Verify it's a copy, not a reference\n    expect(snap).not.toBe(state);\n  });\n\n  it('selectScoeStatistics returns only counter fields', async createCommandIngressState(testGlobalConfig);\n\n    writer.incrementReceiveCount();\n    writer.incrementSuccessCount();\n    writer.incrementErrorCount('fail');\n\n    const stats =   "old_string": "    async consume(events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[]): Promise<ConsumerResult> {\n      const consumed: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];\n      const remaining: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];\n\n      for (const event of events) {\n        if (canHandle(event)) {\n          parse(event);\n          consumed.push(event);\n        } else {\n          remaining.push(event);\n        }\n      }\n\n      return { consumed, remaining };\n    },",
  "new_string": "    async consume(events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[]): Promise<ConsumerResult> {\n      const consumed: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];\n      const remaining: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];\n\n      for (const event of events) {\n        if (canHandle(event)) {\n          try {\n            parse(event);\n          } catch {\n            // Parse failure (checksum, unknown command) — still consume the event\n            // to prevent it from falling through to receive\n          }\n          consumed.push(event);\n        } else {\n          remaining.push(event);\n        }\n      }\n\n      return { consumed, remaining };\n    },"
}

| 文件 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| `rewrite/src/runtime/consumer-chain.ts` | TransportEventConsumer + ConsumerResult 接口 (C1) |
| `rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/types.ts` | 全部领域类型：ScoeGlobalConfig、ScoeCommandConfig、枚举等 (C4) |
| `rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/protocol-adapter.ts` | ProtocolAdapter 接口 + ParsedCommand (C2) |

| `rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/state.ts` | CommandIngressState(13 字段) + Reader/Writer 分离 + tickSecond (C4-C5) |
| `rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/index.ts` | core barrel export |
| `rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/selectors/` | 5 个 selector 返回 readonly 深拷贝快照 (C6) |
| `rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter.ts` | 完整 SCOE 协议解析：canHandle(两阶段状态机) + parse(功能码/校验和/参数 resolve) + consume (C7-C11) |
| `rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/adapters/northbound-protocol-adapter.ts` | canHandle 返回 false 的 stub (C26) |
| `rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/adapters/index.ts` | adapters barrel export |
| `rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/index.ts` | public API 导出 |
| `rewrite/scripts/migrate-scoe-config.ts` | 迁移脚本 + operator 枚举映射 (C22-C23) |
| `rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/fixtures/` | 测试 fixtures |
| `rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/__tests__/` | 23 个 fixture 测试 |

Check field mapping logic for frameInstances → ScoeCommandFrameMapping.\n\n6. **Code quality**: Any code smells, unnecessary complexity, missing edge cases?\n\n7. **Selector immutability**: Do all selectors return deep copies (not references to internal state)?\n\n8. **Test coverage**: Are the tests meaningful? Do they cover the key acceptance criteria (C8 function code, C9 checksum, C10 param resolve, C11 two-phase state machine)?\n\nReport findings with severity: **critical** (wrong behavior), **major** (deviation from design), **minor** (style/quality), **suggestion** (optional improvement)."
}

Check field mapping logic for frameInstances → ScoeCommandFrameMapping.\n\n6. **Code quality**: Any code smells, unnecessary complexity, missing edge cases?\n\n7. **Selector immutability**: Do all selectors return deep copies (not references to internal state)?\n\n8. **Test coverage**: Are the tests meaningful? Do they cover the key acceptance criteria (C8 function code, C9 checksum, C10 param resolve, C11 two-phase state machine)?\n\nReport findings with severity: **critical** (wrong behavior), **major** (deviation from design), **minor** (style/quality), **suggestion** (optional improvement)."

## Code Review Summary

**Files Reviewed:** 14
**Total Issues:** 14

### By Severity
- CRITICAL: 1 (wrong behavior)
- HIGH: 3 (design deviation or type error)
- MEDIUM: 5 (quality/correctness concerns)
- LOW: 5 (style/optional)

---

### Issues

---

**[CRITICAL] consume() discards parsed command result without dispatching**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter.ts:84-90`

Issue: `consume()` calls `parse(event)` but discards the return value. Per design section 2.2.1, the flow is: `canHandle` -> `parse` -> `await dispatch(parsed)`. The W1 implementation calls parse only for its side-effect (throwing on failure), but the parsed command is never stored, never returned, and never dispatched to a handler. This means the entire SCOE command handling pipeline is a no-op. Even for W1 scope, the `ParsedCommand` should at minimum be captured for the consumer's caller to use, or the `consume` return type needs to communicate what was parsed.

```typescript
// Current (line 86):
parse(event);  // result discarded
```

Fix: Capture the parsed command, and either (a) store it in a collection for the consumer's caller to retrieve, or (b) if W1 intentionally defers dispatch, add a comment explaining the design intent and return parsed commands as part of `ConsumerResult` or via a separate mechanism. At minimum, the `consume` result should be enriched so callers know *what* was consumed (not just that an event was consumed).

---

**[HIGH] TypeScript compilation errors in scoe-protocol-adapter.ts**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter.ts:42,53`

Issue: `tsc --noEmit` reports two TS2345 errors. `bytes[fcOffset + 1]` has type `number | undefined` (because `bytes` is `readonly number[]` and the index access can be out of bounds in strict mode), but `byteToHex()` expects `number`. Same issue on line 53 inside `parse()`.

```
error TS2345: Argument of type 'number | undefined' is not assignable to parameter of type 'number'.
```

Fix: Add a non-null assertion or bounds guard:
```typescript
const commandCode = byteToHex(bytes[fcOffset + 1]!);
// or better:
const codeByte = bytes[fcOffset + 1];
if (codeByte === undefined) return false; // in canHandle
if (codeByte === undefined) throw new Error('...'); // in parse
```

---

**[HIGH] TypeScript compilation errors in test file**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/__tests__/scoe-protocol-adapter.spec.ts:129,150,156,210`

Issue: `sendFrameCommandConfig.checksums[0]` returns `ChecksumConfig | undefined` but is passed where `ChecksumConfig` is expected. Line 156 also has `'cs' is possibly 'undefined'`. Four TS errors total.

Fix: Add a non-null assertion since the fixture is controlled:
```typescript
const cs = sendFrameCommandConfig.checksums[0]!;
```

---

**[HIGH] ScoeCommandFunction implemented as `const` array, not `enum`**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/types.ts:5-12`

Issue: The design document section 3.2 explicitly specifies `enum ScoeCommandFunction { LOAD_SATELLITE_ID = 'load_satellite_id', ... }`. The implementation uses `const SCOE_COMMAND_FUNCTIONS = [...] as const` with a derived type instead. While functionally equivalent for runtime, this is a design deviation. The design's handlerMap in section 4.2 uses `ScoeCommandFunction.LOAD_SATELLITE_ID` as map keys (enum member syntax), which does not work with the current `const` array approach.

Fix: Either update the implementation to use `enum ScoeCommandFunction` to match the design, or document the deviation and update the design to reflect the `as const` pattern.

---

**[MEDIUM] Selector `selectCommandIngressSnapshot` returns shallow copy, not deep copy**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/selectors/command-ingress-selectors.ts:23-40`

Issue: The selector creates a new object via spread `{ ... }` but only copies scalar values. While this specific state has no nested objects (all fields are primitives or `readonly` arrays/strings), the function signature promises `ReadonlyDeep<CommandIngressSnapshot>`. The `getSnapshot()` in state.ts also uses `{ ...state }` which does not deep-copy `activeCommandConfigs` (an array of objects). If a caller mutates a nested field of `activeCommandConfigs`, it would corrupt internal state. This violates the CLAUDE.md Selector Immutability Constraint.

Fix: Use `structuredClone()` or a deep-clone utility for `getSnapshot()`:
```typescript
getSnapshot() {
  return structuredClone(state);
}
```
Or at minimum, ensure `activeCommandConfigs` is deep-copied in the snapshot.

---

**[MEDIUM] `getSnapshot()` returns shallow spread, not immutable deep copy**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/state.ts:74-76`

Issue: `return { ...state }` creates a shallow copy. The `activeCommandConfigs` field is `readonly ScoeCommandConfig[]`, which contains objects. The spread does not deep-copy these objects, meaning a consumer could potentially mutate the config objects through the snapshot reference (since `readonly` is only compile-time).

Fix: Use `structuredClone(state)` for the snapshot, or manually deep-copy the `activeCommandConfigs` array.

---

**[MEDIUM] consume() silently swallows parse errors**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter.ts:87-89`

Issue: When `parse(event)` throws (checksum failure, unknown command code), the error is caught by an empty `catch {}` block. The event is still pushed to `consumed`. This means a malformed SCOE command is silently consumed with no logging, no state update, and no feedback to the caller. The design section 2.2.1 says "parse(event)" and "await dispatch(parsed)" -- there is no provision for silent failure. At minimum, the state should track this as an error (`incrementErrorCount`).

Fix: At minimum, log the error or increment error count. Ideally, communicate parse failures to the caller:
```typescript
try {
  const parsed = parse(event);
  // dispatch or store parsed
} catch (err) {
  // Track parse failure, don't silently swallow
  console.warn(`SCOE parse error: ${(err as Error).message}`);
}
```

---

**[MEDIUM] `resetRuntimeState()` also resets `activeCommandConfigs` but design section 4.3.2 does not mention it**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/state.ts:100-115`

Issue: `resetRuntimeState()` clears `activeCommandConfigs` to `[]`. The design section 4.3.2 (handleUnloadSatelliteId) lists the fields reset by UNLOAD, and `activeCommandConfigs` is not in that list. This may be intentional (clearing configs on unload makes sense), but it is a design deviation.

Fix: If intentional, update the design document to include `activeCommandConfigs` in the UNLOAD reset list. If unintentional, remove `activeCommandConfigs: []` from `resetRuntimeState()`.

---

**[MEDIUM] State `getSnapshot()` does not freeze the returned object**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/state.ts:74-76`

Issue: Per CLAUDE.md "Selector 不可变约束", feature selector return values must be "readonly 类型 + 深拷贝或冻结对象". The current implementation returns a shallow spread without `Object.freeze()`. This is inconsistent with the project's stated constraint.

Fix: Either use `structuredClone()` or `Object.freeze()` on the returned snapshot, matching the approach used by other features in this project.

---

**[LOW] `updateStatus(patch)` accepts `Partial<CommandIngressState>`, allowing `globalConfig` to be overwritten**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/state.ts:117-119`

Issue: `updateStatus({ ...state, ...patch })` can overwrite any field including `globalConfig` and `activeCommandConfigs`. This is a very broad API. If a caller passes `globalConfig` in the patch, it would silently replace the config. The design's `updateStatus` is intended for runtime status fields only.

Fix: Consider restricting the patch type to exclude config fields:
```typescript
type RuntimeStatusPatch = Partial<Pick<CommandIngressState,
  'healthStatus' | 'linkTestResult' | 'lastCommandCode' | 'receiveCommandSuccess' | 'lastErrorReason'>>;
```

---

**[LOW] `parse()` uses `event.bytes!` non-null assertion without prior check**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter.ts:50`

Issue: `parse()` is called after `canHandle()` has verified `event.bytes !== undefined`, but `parse()` itself uses `event.bytes!` without any assertion. If `parse()` is ever called independently (not from `consume()`), this would be unsafe. This is a minor robustness concern.

Fix: Add an explicit guard at the top of `parse()`:
```typescript
if (event.bytes === undefined) throw new Error('Event has no bytes');
```

---

**[LOW] Tests for C10 (param resolve) do not actually verify resolved params**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/__tests__/scoe-protocol-adapter.spec.ts:192-221`

Issue: The test comments say "W1: consume only categorizes, doesn't parse" and the test only verifies that the event is consumed. It does not actually test that parameter resolution works correctly. The test has a comment explaining the limitation, but the C10 acceptance criterion ("param resolve") cannot be verified this way.

Fix: When dispatch is implemented, add a proper test that verifies `resolvedParams` contains the correct mapped values. Alternatively, export `extractAndResolveParams` for direct unit testing.

---

**[LOW] Migration script uses duplicated type definitions instead of importing from the feature**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/scripts/migrate-scoe-config.ts:122-186`

Issue: The script re-defines `ComparisonOperator`, `NewFieldMapping`, `NewScoeCommandFrameMapping`, etc. instead of importing from `@/features/command-ingress/core/types`. This means if the types change in the feature, the migration script could become out of sync.

Fix: This is acceptable for a one-time migration script that must run standalone outside the build system. No change needed, but a comment noting this intentional duplication would help future maintainers.

---

**[LOW] `protocolId` is `'scoe-tcp'` but the protocol can also receive events via other transports**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter.ts:77`

Issue: The `protocolId` is hardcoded as `'scoe-tcp'`. This is fine for W1 but may need to be configurable or at least documented if SCOE events arrive via UDP in future.

Fix: No action needed for W1. Consider making this configurable in later waves.

---

**[SUGGESTION] Barrel export from `adapters/index.ts` does not re-export `ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions` as a type**

File: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/adapters/index.ts:2`

Issue: `ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions` is exported with `export type` which is correct. No issue here, just noting the pattern is consistent.

---

### Positive Observations

1. **Clean type structure**: All domain types in `core/types.ts` use `readonly` consistently. The type hierarchy (GlobalConfig -> CommandConfig -> FrameMapping -> FieldMapping) is well-layered and matches the design document precisely.

2. **State Reader/Writer separation**: The `CommandIngressStateContainer` pattern with separated read/write interfaces is clean and follows the project's established pattern. The factory function correctly encapsulates state via closure.

3. **tickSecond logic matches design exactly**: The two-branch logic (if `!scoeFramesLoaded` clear stats, else increment `satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds`) is a faithful implementation of design section 8.3.

4. **Fixture design**: Test fixtures are well-structured with reusable builders (`buildScoeFrame`, `buildScoeFrameWithChecksum`, `createFakeStateReader`) that make tests readable.

5. **Migration script operator mapping**: The `OPERATOR_MAP` in `migrate-scoe-config.ts` matches the design section 9.5 table exactly (7 mappings, all correct).

6. **NorthboundProtocolAdapter stub**: Clean stub that passes all events to `remaining`, matching design C26.

7. **Consumer chain interface**: `TransportEventConsumer` and `ConsumerResult` are minimal and well-defined, matching design section 1.1 exactly.

8. **Two-phase state machine tests**: The C11 tests cover both phases correctly and test the critical scenario of phase 1 rejecting non-LOAD commands.

---

### Recommendation

**REQUEST CHANGES**

There is one CRITICAL issue (consumed events produce no side effects -- the entire pipeline is a no-op since parsed commands are discarded) and three HIGH issues (TypeScript compilation errors and a design deviation in enum vs const). These must be resolved before this code can be considered correct and ready for subsequent waves.

1	import type { ScoeGlobalConfig, ScoeCommandConfig, HealthStatus, LinkTestResult } from './types';
2	
3	// --- State ---
4	
5	export interface CommandIngressState {
6	  readonly commandReceiveCount: number;
7	  readonly commandSuccessCount: number;
8	  readonly commandErrorCount: number;
9	
10	  readonly loadedSatelliteId: string;
11	  readonly scoeFramesLoaded: boolean;
12	  readonly healthStatus: HealthStatus;
13	  readonly linkTestResult: LinkTestResult;
14	  readonly lastCommandCode: string;
15	  readonly receiveCommandSuccess: boolean;
16	
17	  readonly runtimeSeconds: number;
18	  readonly satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: number;
19	  readonly lastErrorReason: string;
20	
21	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig;
22	  readonly activeCommandConfigs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[];
23	}
24	
25	// --- Reader ---
26	
27	export interface CommandIngressStateReader {
28	  getSnapshot(): CommandIngressState;
29	  globalConfig(): ScoeGlobalConfig;
30	  readonly loadedSatelliteId: string;
31	  readonly scoeFramesLoaded: boolean;
32	}
33	
34	// --- Writer ---
35	
36	export interface CommandIngressStateWriter {
37	  setLoaded(satelliteId: string, configs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[]): void;
38	  resetRuntimeState(): void;
39	  updateStatus(patch: Partial<CommandIngressState>): void;
40	  incrementReceiveCount(): void;
41	  incrementSuccessCount(): void;
42	  incrementErrorCount(reason: string): void;
43	  tickSecond(): void;
44	}
45	
46	// --- Factory ---
47	
48	export interface CommandIngressStateContainer {
49	  readonly reader: CommandIngressStateReader;
50	  readonly writer: CommandIngressStateWriter;
51	}
52	
53	export function createCommandIngressState(
54	  globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig,
55	): CommandIngressStateContainer {
56	  let state: CommandIngressState = {
57	    commandReceiveCount: 0,
58	    commandSuccessCount: 0,
59	    commandErrorCount: 0,
60	    loadedSatelliteId: '',
61	    scoeFramesLoaded: false,
62	    healthStatus: 'unknown',
63	    linkTestResult: 'unknown',
64	    lastCommandCode: '',
65	    receiveCommandSuccess: false,
66	    runtimeSeconds: 0,
67	    satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,
68	    lastErrorReason: '',
69	    globalConfig,
70	    activeCommandConfigs: [],
71	  };
72	
73	  const reader: CommandIngressStateReader = {
74	    getSnapshot() {
75	      return { ...state };
76	    },
77	    globalConfig() {
78	      return state.globalConfig;
79	    },
80	    get loadedSatelliteId() {
81	      return state.loadedSatelliteId;
82	    },
83	    get scoeFramesLoaded() {
84	      return state.scoeFramesLoaded;
85	    },
86	  };
87	
88	  const writer: CommandIngressStateWriter = {
89	    setLoaded(satelliteId, configs) {
90	      state = {
91	        ...state,
92	        loadedSatelliteId: satelliteId,
93	        scoeFramesLoaded: true,
94	        satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,
95	        activeCommandConfigs: [...configs],
96	      };
97	    },
98	
99	    resetRuntimeState() {
100	      state = {
101	        ...state,
102	        scoeFramesLoaded: false,
103	        loadedSatelliteId: '',
104	        satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,
105	        commandReceiveCount: 0,
106	        commandSuccessCount: 0,
107	        commandErrorCount: 0,
108	        healthStatus: 'unknown',
109	        linkTestResult: 'unknown',
110	        receiveCommandSuccess: false,
111	        lastCommandCode: '',
112	        lastErrorReason: '',
113	        activeCommandConfigs: [],
114	      };
115	    },
116	
117	    updateStatus(patch) {
118	      state = { ...state, ...patch };
119	    },
120	
121	    incrementReceiveCount() {
122	      state = {
123	        ...state,
124	        commandReceiveCount: state.commandReceiveCount + 1,
125	      };
126	    },
127	
128	    incrementSuccessCount() {
129	      state = {
130	        ...state,
131	        commandSuccessCount: state.commandSuccessCount + 1,
132	        receiveCommandSuccess: true,
133	      };
134	    },
135	
136	    incrementErrorCount(reason) {
137	      state = {
138	        ...state,
139	        commandErrorCount: state.commandErrorCount + 1,
140	        lastErrorReason: reason,
141	        receiveCommandSuccess: false,
142	      };
143	    },
144	
145	    tickSecond() {
146	      let next = {
147	        ...state,
148	        runtimeSeconds: state.runtimeSeconds + 1,
149	        receiveCommandSuccess: false,
150	      };
151	
152	      if (!next.scoeFramesLoaded) {
153	        next = {
154	          ...next,
155	          commandReceiveCount: 0,
156	          commandSuccessCount: 0,
157	          commandErrorCount: 0,
158	          healthStatus: 'unknown',
159	          linkTestResult: 'unknown',
160	        };
161	      } else {
162	        next = {
163	          ...next,
164	          satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: next.satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds + 1,
165	        };
166	      }
167	
168	      state = next;
169	    },
170	  };
171	
172	  return { reader, writer };
173	}

1	import type { ProtocolAdapter, ParsedCommand } from '../core';
2	import type {
3	  ScoeGlobalConfig,
4	  ScoeCommandConfig,
5	  ChecksumConfig,
6	  ScoeCommandParam,
7	} from '../core';
8	import type { CommandIngressStateReader } from '../core';
9	import type { TransportEventSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
10	import type { ConsumerResult } from '@/runtime/consumer-chain';
11	
12	export interface ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions {
13	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig;
14	  readonly commandConfigs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[];
15	  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;
16	}
17	
18	export function createScoeProtocolAdapter(
19	  options: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions,
20	): ProtocolAdapter {
21	  const { globalConfig, stateReader } = options;
22	  const commandConfigs = [...options.commandConfigs];
23	
24	  function canHandle(event: TransportEventSnapshot): boolean {
25	    if (event.kind !== 'data' || event.bytes === undefined) return false;
26	
27	    const bytes = event.bytes;
28	    const fcOffset = globalConfig.functionCodeOffset;
29	
30	    if (bytes.length < fcOffset + 4) return false;
31	
32	    const identifierByte = bytes[fcOffset];
33	    const expectedIdentifier = hexToByte(globalConfig.scoeIdentifier);
34	    if (expectedIdentifier === null || identifierByte !== expectedIdentifier) return false;
35	
36	    // Fixed 0xAA 0xAA at offset +2 and +3
37	    if (bytes[fcOffset + 2] !== 0xaa || bytes[fcOffset + 3] !== 0xaa) return false;
38	
39	    // Two-phase state machine
40	    if (!stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {
41	      // Phase 1: only match LOAD_SATELLITE_ID (command code 01)
42	      const commandCode = byteToHex(bytes[fcOffset + 1]);
43	      if (commandCode !== '01') return false;
44	    }
45	
46	    return true;
47	  }
48	
49	  function parse(event: TransportEventSnapshot): ParsedCommand {
50	    const bytes = event.bytes!;
51	    const fcOffset = globalConfig.functionCodeOffset;
52	
53	    const commandCode = byteToHex(bytes[fcOffset + 1]);
54	    const commandConfig = commandConfigs.find((c) => c.code === commandCode);
55	    if (!commandConfig) {
56	      throw new Error(`Unknown SCOE command code: ${commandCode}`);
57	    }
58	
59	    validateChecksums(bytes, commandConfig.checksums);
60	
61	    const resolvedParams = extractAndResolveParams(bytes, commandConfig.params ?? []);
62	
63	    return {
64	      commandId: `scoe-cmd-${event.id}`,
65	      commandCode,
66	      commandFunction: commandConfig.function,
67	      rawBytes: [...bytes],
68	      resolvedParams,
69	      commandConfig,
70	      connectionId: event.connectionId,
71	      occurredAt: event.occurredAt,
72	    };
73	  }
74	
75	  return {
76	    id: 'scoe-protocol',
77	    protocolId: 'scoe-tcp',
78	
79	    async consume(events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[]): Promise<ConsumerResult> {
80	      const consumed: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];
81	      const remaining: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];
82	
83	      for (const event of events) {
84	        if (canHandle(event)) {
85	          try {
86	            parse(event);
87	          } catch {
88	            // Parse failure (checksum, unknown command) — still consume the event
89	            // to prevent it from falling through to receive
90	          }
91	          consumed.push(event);
92	        } else {
93	          remaining.push(event);
94	        }
95	      }
96	
97	      return { consumed, remaining };
98	    },
99	  };
100	}
101	
102	// --- Byte-level helpers ---
103	
104	function byteToHex(byte: number): string {
105	  return byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0').toUpperCase();
106	}
107	
108	function hexToByte(hex: string): number | null {
109	  const cleaned = hex.replace(/^0x/i, '');
110	  const value = parseInt(cleaned, 16);
111	  return Number.isNaN(value) ? null : value;
112	}
113	
114	function validateChecksums(
115	  bytes: readonly number[],
116	  checksums: readonly ChecksumConfig[],
117	): void {
118	  for (const cs of checksums) {
119	    if (!cs.enabled) continue;
120	
121	    let sum = 0;
122	    for (let i = cs.offset; i < cs.offset + cs.length; i++) {
123	      sum += bytes[i] ?? 0;
124	    }
125	    const expected = sum % 256;
126	    const actual = bytes[cs.checksumOffset] ?? 0;
127	
128	    if (expected !== actual) {
129	      throw new Error(
130	        `Checksum failed at offset ${cs.checksumOffset}: expected ${expected}, got ${actual}`,
131	      );
132	    }
133	  }
134	}
135	
136	function extractAndResolveParams(
137	  bytes: readonly number[],
138	  params: readonly ScoeCommandParam[],
139	): Record<string, string> {
140	  const result: Record<string, string> = {};
141	
142	  for (const param of params) {
143	    const slice = bytes.slice(param.offset, param.offset + param.length);
144	    const hex = bytesToUpperCaseHex(slice);
145	    const normalized = normalizeHex(hex, param.length * 2);
146	
147	    const matched = param.options.find(
148	      (opt) => normalizeHex(opt.receiveCode, param.length * 2) === normalized,
149	    );
150	
151	    result[param.id] = matched?.value ?? hex;
152	  }
153	
154	  return result;
155	}
156	
157	function bytesToUpperCaseHex(bytes: readonly number[]): string {
158	  return Array.from(bytes)
159	    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0').toUpperCase())
160	    .join('');
161	}
162	
163	function normalizeHex(hex: string, targetLength: number): string {
164	  const cleaned = hex.replace(/^0x/i, '').toUpperCase();
165	  return cleaned.padStart(targetLength, '0');
166	}
167	
```

{

  "old_string": "describe('extractAndResolveParams – direct unit tests', () => {\n  it('resolves param by matching receiveCode hex to option value', () => {\n    const params = sendFrameCommandConfig.params!;\n    const bytes = buildScoeFrame(0x05, [\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x02, // param at offset 13, length 1 → hex \"02\" → receiveCode \"02\" → value \"mode_b\"\n    ]);\n\n    const result = extractAndResolveParams(bytes, params);\n    expect(result['param-mode']).toBe('mode_b');\n  });\n\n  it('resolves to first option when hex matches 01', () => {\n    const params = sendFrameCommandConfig.params!;\n    const bytes = buildScoeFrame(0x05, [\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x01, // hex \"01\" → receiveCode \"01\" → value \"mode_a\"\n    ]);\n\n    const result = extractAndResolveParams(bytes, params);\n    expect(result['param-mode']).toBe('mode_a');\n  });\n\n  it('falls back to raw hex when no option matches', () => {\n    const params = sendFrameCommandConfig.params!;\n    const bytes = buildScoeFrame(0x05, [\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0xff, // hex \"FF\" → no matching option → raw \"FF\"\n    ]);\n\n    const result = extractAndResolveParams(bytes, params);\n    expect(result['param-mode']).toBe('FF');\n  });",
  "new_string": "describe('extractAndResolveParams – direct unit tests', () => {\n  it('resolves param by matching receiveCode hex to option value', () => {\n    const params = sendFrameCommandConfig.params!;\n    // Frame: [0x01, 0x05, 0xaa, 0xaa, ...9 zeros..., 0x02] = 14 bytes\n    // param offset=13 → bytes[13] = 0x02 → hex \"02\" → receiveCode \"02\" → value \"mode_b\"\n    const bytes = buildScoeFrame(0x05, [\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00, 0x02,\n    ]);\n\n    const result = extractAndResolveParams(bytes, params);\n    expect(result['param-mode']).toBe('mode_b');\n  });\n\n  it('resolves to first option when hex matches 01', () => {\n    const params = sendFrameCommandConfig.params!;\n    const bytes = buildScoeFrame(0x05, [\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00, 0x01,\n    ]);\n\n    const result = extractAndResolveParams(bytes, params);\n    expect(result['param-mode']).toBe('mode_a');\n  });\n\n  it('falls back to raw hex when no option matches', () => {\n    const params = sendFrameCommandConfig.params!;\n    const bytes = buildScoeFrame(0x05, [\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,\n      0x00, 0xff,\n    ]);\n\n    const result = extractAndResolveParams(bytes, params);\n    expect(result['param-mode']).toBe('FF');\n  });"
}

全部绿色。审查修复完成。

**审查修复总结：**

| 修复项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| TS 安全 | `parse()` 加 bytes guard + `byteToHex` 加 non-null assertion |
| 深拷贝 | `getSnapshot()` 改用 `structuredClone(state)` |
| 类型收窄 | `updateStatus` patch 类型改为 `Omit<..., 'globalConfig' \| 'activeCommandConfigs'>` |
| 错误可见性 | consume() 的 catch 块加 `console.warn` |
| 测试增强 | 导出 `extractAndResolveParams` / `validateChecksums`，新增 6 个直接单元测试 |
| W1 设计意图 | consume() 加注释说明 W1 只做分类验证，W2 加 dispatch |

**不修改的审查项（有意选择）：**
- enum vs `as const`：项目中一致用 `as const` 模式
- resetRuntimeState 清 activeCommandConfigs：UNLOAD 设计上就该清
- 迁移脚本重复类型：一次性脚本，可接受
- protocolId 硬编码：W1 scope

**Verify evidence:**
- `pnpm lint` → 0 errors (exit 0)
- `quasar build` → Build succeeded
- `vitest run` → 29 PASS, 0 FAIL
